   2  0000              ; Build NeoGS firmware and one deployable package from the Project 124 root:
   3  0000              ;   sjasmplus neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm

   5  0000              ; neogs_code.bin       custom firmware, loaded at logical RAM address $0000

# file opened: main.asm
   1+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 0000              ; PROJECT 124 — NeoGS Z80 firmware / command dispatcher
   3+ 0000              ; Based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and ports.inc v0.3.
   4+ 0000              ;
   5+ 0000              ; MEMORY MAP / BOOT:
   6+ 0000              ; The stock GS105a ROM loads a tiny module at $5B00. That module enables
   7+ 0000              ; RAM-over-ROM and receives this firmware at logical RAM address $0000.
   8+ 0000              ; Current firmware image is kept below $3400; the stack grows down from $3FFF.
   9+ 0000              ; PG1 normally maps physical page 1 (register value #03).
  10+ 0000              ; During x86 DMA, PG1 maps physical page 68 (register value #44) at $4000-$7FFF.
  11+ 0000              ; Physical page 68 starts at $110000; firmware and its stack remain in page 0.
  12+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
  13+ 0000
  14+ 0000                      INCLUDE "../shared/neogs_io.inc"
# file opened: neogs_io.inc
   1++0000              ; =========================================================================
   2++0000              ; NeoGS ports.inc v0.3 — Project 124 used subset
   3++0000              ; Hardware port names/addresses from NedoPC ports.inc v0.3.
   4++0000              ; =========================================================================
   5++0000
   6++0000              ; ZX Spectrum side
   7++0000              GSCOM           EQU #BB             ; write command / read status
   8++0000              GSSTAT          EQU #BB             ; read CBIT (bit 0), DBIT (bit 7)
   9++0000              GSDAT           EQU #B3             ; read/write data byte
  10++0000              GSCTR           EQU #33             ; global control
  11++0000
  12++0000              B_CBIT          EQU 0
  13++0000              M_CBIT          EQU #01
  14++0000              B_DBIT          EQU 7
  15++0000              M_DBIT          EQU #80
  16++0000
  17++0000              C_GRST          EQU #80
  18++0000              C_GNMI          EQU #40
  19++0000              C_GLED          EQU #20
  20++0000
  21++0000              ; NeoGS Z80 local ports
  22++0000              MPAG            EQU #00
  23++0000              MPAGEX          EQU #10             ; independent 16 KiB page for C000-FFFF
  24++0000              ZXCMD           EQU #01
  25++0000              ZXDATRD         EQU #02
  26++0000              ZXDATWR         EQU #03
  27++0000              ZXSTAT          EQU #04
  28++0000              CLRCBIT         EQU #05
  29++0000
  30++0000              GSCFG0          EQU #0F
  31++0000              B_NOROM         EQU 0
  32++0000              M_NOROM         EQU #01
  33++0000              B_RAMRO         EQU 1
  34++0000              M_RAMRO         EQU #02
  35++0000              B_8CHANS        EQU 2
  36++0000              M_8CHANS        EQU #04
  37++0000              B_EXPAG         EQU 3
  38++0000              M_EXPAG         EQU #08
  39++0000              B_CKSEL0        EQU 4
  40++0000              M_CKSEL0        EQU #10
  41++0000              B_CKSEL1        EQU 5
  42++0000              M_CKSEL1        EQU #20
  43++0000              C_10MHZ         EQU #30
  44++0000              C_12MHZ         EQU #10
  45++0000              C_20MHZ         EQU #20
  46++0000              C_24MHZ         EQU #00
  47++0000              B_PAN4CH        EQU 6
  48++0000              M_PAN4CH        EQU #40
  49++0000              B_INV7B         EQU 7
  50++0000              M_INV7B         EQU #80
  51++0000
  52++0000              ; NeoGS physical page registers
  53++0000              PG0             EQU #20
  54++0000              PG1             EQU #21
  55++0000              PG2             EQU #22
  56++0000              PG3             EQU #23
  57++0000
  58++0000              ; NeoGS DMA registers
  59++0000              DMA_MOD         EQU #1B
  60++0000              DMA_HAD         EQU #1C
  61++0000              DMA_MAD         EQU #1D
  62++0000              DMA_LAD         EQU #1E
  63++0000              DMA_CST         EQU #1F
  64++0000              C_DMA_NONE      EQU 0
  65++0000              C_DMA_ZX        EQU 1
  66++0000              C_DMA_SD        EQU 2
  67++0000              C_DMA_MP3       EQU 3
  68++0000
  69++0000              ; Standard General Sound ROM commands.
  70++0000              GS_CMD_RESET_FLAGS  EQU #00
  71++0000              GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM EQU #20
  72++0000              GS_CMD_LOAD_MODULE  EQU #30
  73++0000              GS_CMD_RUN_MODULE   EQU #31
  74++0000              GS_CMD_OPEN_STREAM  EQU #D1
  75++0000              GS_CMD_CLOSE_STREAM EQU #D2
  76++0000              GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART EQU #F4
  77++0000              GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS EQU #13
  78++0000              GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK EQU #14
  79++0000
  80++0000              ; Project 124 private commands carried by GSCOM after custom firmware starts.
  81++0000              CMD_RESET       EQU #01
  82++0000              CMD_LOAD_X86    EQU #02
  83++0000              CMD_RUN_X86     EQU #03
  84++0000              CMD_GET_STATUS  EQU #04
  85++0000              CMD_MEM_TEST    EQU #05
  86++0000              CMD_BIOS_POST   EQU #07
  87++0000              CMD_INT_TEST    EQU #08
  88++0000              CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST EQU #09
  89++0000              CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST EQU #0A
  90++0000              CMD_RUN_X86_REAL EQU #0B
  91++0000
  92++0000              ; Project 124 startup handshake bytes.
  93++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK EQU #A1
  94++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK EQU #A3
  95++0000              NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK  EQU #A2
  96++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK EQU #E1
  97++0000              NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE   EQU #A5
  98++0000
  99++0000              NGS_STATUS_CBIT EQU M_CBIT
 100++0000              NGS_STATUS_DBIT EQU M_DBIT
 101++0000
 102++0000              ; The x86 DMA frame occupies logical $4000..$7FFF (16 KiB) via PG1.
 103++0000              ; PG1 direct page value #44 selects physical RAM page 68 ($110000).
 104++0000              ; The two-byte length header leaves #3FFE bytes for the guest image.
 105++0000              NGS_X86_DMA_WINDOW_SIZE EQU #4000
 106++0000              NGS_X86_MAX_CODE_SIZE   EQU #3FFE

  15+ 0000
  16+ 0000                      ORG     $0000
  17+ 0000
  18+ 0000              ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR EQU $4000
  19+ 0000              X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR        EQU $4000
  20+ 0000              X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR           EQU $4002
  21+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
  22+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_OUTER   EQU 8
  23+ 0000              IPC_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF

  25+ 0000              ; Firmware entry vector
  26+ 0000 C3 00 01             jp      start_neoprogram
  27+ 0003 00 00 00...          BLOCK   53, 0
  28+ 0038 FB                   ei
  29+ 0039 C9                   ret
  30+ 003A 00 00 00...          BLOCK   198, 0
  31+ 0100
  32+ 0100              start_neoprogram:
  33+ 0100 F3                   di
  34+ 0101 ED 56                im      1
  35+ 0103 31 FF 3F             ld      sp, $3FFF
  36+ 0106
  37+ 0106                      ; Set Z80 to the documented maximum guaranteed clock: 20 MHz.
  38+ 0106                      ; Do not select 24 MHz: the guide explicitly says it is not guaranteed.
  39+ 0106 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  40+ 0108 E6 CF                and     #CF
  41+ 010A F6 20                or      C_20MHZ
  42+ 010C D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  43+ 010E
  44+ 010E AF                   xor     a
  45+ 010F 32 B2 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
  46+ 0112 32 B6 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
  47+ 0115 32 B7 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
  48+ 0118 32 B8 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
  49+ 011B 32 B9 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
  50+ 011E 32 04 0E             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  51+ 0121 32 B0 04             ld      (neo_command), a
  52+ 0124 32 B1 04             ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
  53+ 0127
  54+ 0127                      ; Copy the ROM-return trampoline into unoverlaid RAM at $4000.
  55+ 0127 21 BA 04             ld      hl, rom_return_trampoline_code
  56+ 012A 11 00 40             ld      de, ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
  57+ 012D 01 09 00             ld      bc, rom_return_trampoline_end - rom_return_trampoline_code
  58+ 0130 ED B0                ldir
  59+ 0132
  60+ 0132                      ; Notify the Spectrum only after the new firmware is executing.
  61+ 0132 3E A2                ld      a, NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
  62+ 0134 CD 83 04             call    neo_send_data_byte
  63+ 0137
  64+ 0137              main_loop:
  65+ 0137                      ; A command exists only while CBIT (ZXSTAT bit 0) is set.
  66+ 0137 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
  67+ 0139 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
  68+ 013B 28 FA                jr      z, main_loop
  69+ 013D
  70+ 013D                      ; Read the command byte from ZXCMD. Reading it does NOT clear CBIT.
  71+ 013D DB 01                in      a, (ZXCMD)
  72+ 013F 32 B0 04             ld      (neo_command), a
  73+ 0142
  74+ 0142                      ; Standard GS #F4 cold restart also returns this custom firmware to ROM.
  75+ 0142 FE F4                cp      GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
  76+ 0144 CA 7F 01             jp      z, command_return_to_gsrom
  77+ 0147 FE 01                cp      CMD_RESET
  78+ 0149 CA 87 01             jp      z, command_reset
  79+ 014C FE 02                cp      CMD_LOAD_X86
  80+ 014E CA A4 01             jp      z, command_load_x86
  81+ 0151 FE 03                cp      CMD_RUN_X86
  82+ 0153 CA 90 02             jp      z, command_run_x86
  83+ 0156 FE 04                cp      CMD_GET_STATUS
  84+ 0158 CA E2 03             jp      z, command_get_status
  85+ 015B FE 05                cp      CMD_MEM_TEST
  86+ 015D CA A7 03             jp      z, command_mem_test
  87+ 0160 FE 07                cp      CMD_BIOS_POST
  88+ 0162 CA B0 03             jp      z, command_bios_post
  89+ 0165 FE 08                cp      CMD_INT_TEST
  90+ 0167 CA B9 03             jp      z, command_int_test
  91+ 016A FE 09                cp      CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
  92+ 016C CA C2 03             jp      z, command_int_guest_test
  93+ 016F FE 0A                cp      CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
  94+ 0171 CA CB 03             jp      z, command_bios_video_test
  95+ 0174 FE 0B                cp      CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
  96+ 0176 CA D4 03             jp      z, command_run_x86_real
  97+ 0179
  98+ 0179                      ; Unknown command: release CBIT so the host cannot deadlock.
  99+ 0179 AF                   xor     a
 100+ 017A D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 101+ 017C C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 102+ 017F
 103+ 017F              ; A #F4 command is also the standard GS105a cold-restart command.
 104+ 017F              ; If our firmware is running, reveal the stock ROM again and jump to its
 105+ 017F              ; reset vector. Leave CBIT asserted: the ROM's cold-start path clears it when
 106+ 017F              ; the standard firmware is ready, so the host's long wait is a real handshake.
 107+ 017F              command_return_to_gsrom:
 108+ 017F 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 109+ 0181 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore trampoline's physical page
 110+ 0184 C3 00 40             jp      ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
 111+ 0187
 112+ 0187              command_reset:
 113+ 0187 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 114+ 0189 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; ensure firmware page 1 is visible
 115+ 018C AF                   xor     a
 116+ 018D 32 B2 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 117+ 0190 32 04 0E             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 118+ 0193 32 B6 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
 119+ 0196 32 B7 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
 120+ 0199 32 B8 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
 121+ 019C 32 B9 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
 122+ 019F D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 123+ 01A1 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 124+ 01A4
 125+ 01A4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 126+ 01A4              ; CMD_LOAD_X86
 127+ 01A4              ; 1) Map physical page 68 at logical $4000-$7FFF through PG1=#44.
 128+ 01A4              ; 2) Select ZX DMA module and set physical DMA target $110000.
 129+ 01A4              ; 3) Enable DMA by setting DMA_CST bit 7.
 130+ 01A4              ; 4) Clear CBIT: this is the READY indication to the host.
 131+ 01A4              ; 5) Wait for host's end marker (#FF written to GSCOM, setting CBIT).
 132+ 01A4              ; 6) Disable DMA, then clear CBIT to acknowledge completion.
 133+ 01A4              ;
 134+ 01A4              ; Host uses OUT/LDIR writes into $0000-$3FFF. No dummy write is performed.
 135+ 01A4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 136+ 01A4              command_load_x86:
 137+ 01A4                      ; PG1 is a direct page register: value #44 maps physical page 68.
 138+ 01A4                      ; The DMA engine writes physical $110000; Z80 sees the same bytes at $4000.
 139+ 01A4 3E 44                ld      a, #44
 140+ 01A6 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 141+ 01A9
 142+ 01A9                      ; Robust PG1 test: use different markers in both physical pages.
 143+ 01A9                      ; Save and restore the original byte at $4000 in each page.
 144+ 01A9                      ; MAP bits:
 145+ 01A9                      ; bit 0: page #44 write/read (#A5)
 146+ 01A9                      ; bit 1: page #03 write/read (#5A)
 147+ 01A9                      ; bit 2: page #44 still contains #A5 after writing page #03
 148+ 01A9                      ; bit 3: page #03 still contains #5A after returning from page #44
 149+ 01A9 AF                   xor     a
 150+ 01AA 32 B3 04             ld      (neo_pg1_map_verified), a
 151+ 01AD
 152+ 01AD                      ; Save original byte from normal page #03.
 153+ 01AD 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 154+ 01AF CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 155+ 01B2 3A 00 40             ld      a, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 156+ 01B5 32 B4 04             ld      (neo_pg1_saved_page03), a
 157+ 01B8
 158+ 01B8                      ; Select page #44 and save its original byte.
 159+ 01B8 3E 44                ld      a, #44
 160+ 01BA CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 161+ 01BD 3A 00 40             ld      a, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 162+ 01C0 32 B5 04             ld      (neo_pg1_saved_page44), a
 163+ 01C3
 164+ 01C3                      ; Write and verify marker on page #44.
 165+ 01C3 3E A5                ld      a, #A5
 166+ 01C5 32 00 40             ld      (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR), a
 167+ 01C8 3A 00 40             ld      a, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 168+ 01CB FE A5                cp      #A5
 169+ 01CD 20 08                jr      nz, .pg1_test_page03
 170+ 01CF 3A B3 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_map_verified)
 171+ 01D2 F6 01                or      1
 172+ 01D4 32 B3 04             ld      (neo_pg1_map_verified), a
 173+ 01D7
 174+ 01D7              .pg1_test_page03:
 175+ 01D7                      ; Switch to page #03, write a different marker and verify it.
 176+ 01D7 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 177+ 01D9 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 178+ 01DC 3E 5A                ld      a, #5A
 179+ 01DE 32 00 40             ld      (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR), a
 180+ 01E1 3A 00 40             ld      a, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 181+ 01E4 FE 5A                cp      #5A
 182+ 01E6 20 08                jr      nz, .pg1_test_check_page44
 183+ 01E8 3A B3 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_map_verified)
 184+ 01EB F6 02                or      2
 185+ 01ED 32 B3 04             ld      (neo_pg1_map_verified), a
 186+ 01F0
 187+ 01F0              .pg1_test_check_page44:
 188+ 01F0                      ; Page #44 must retain #A5 despite the write to page #03.
 189+ 01F0 3E 44                ld      a, #44
 190+ 01F2 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 191+ 01F5 3A 00 40             ld      a, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 192+ 01F8 FE A5                cp      #A5
 193+ 01FA 20 08                jr      nz, .pg1_test_check_page03
 194+ 01FC 3A B3 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_map_verified)
 195+ 01FF F6 04                or      4
 196+ 0201 32 B3 04             ld      (neo_pg1_map_verified), a
 197+ 0204
 198+ 0204              .pg1_test_check_page03:
 199+ 0204                      ; Page #03 must retain #5A after switching away and back.
 200+ 0204 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 201+ 0206 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 202+ 0209 3A 00 40             ld      a, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 203+ 020C FE 5A                cp      #5A
 204+ 020E 20 08                jr      nz, .pg1_test_restore_pages
 205+ 0210 3A B3 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_map_verified)
 206+ 0213 F6 08                or      8
 207+ 0215 32 B3 04             ld      (neo_pg1_map_verified), a
 208+ 0218
 209+ 0218              .pg1_test_restore_pages:
 210+ 0218                      ; Restore the original page #44 byte, then the original page #03 byte.
 211+ 0218 3E 44                ld      a, #44
 212+ 021A CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 213+ 021D 3A B5 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_saved_page44)
 214+ 0220 32 00 40             ld      (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR), a
 215+ 0223
 216+ 0223 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 217+ 0225 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 218+ 0228 3A B4 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_saved_page03)
 219+ 022B 32 00 40             ld      (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR), a
 220+ 022E
 221+ 022E 3A B3 04             ld      a, (neo_pg1_map_verified)
 222+ 0231 FE 0F                cp      15
 223+ 0233 28 10                jr      z, .pg1_map_test_done
 224+ 0235
 225+ 0235                      ; On failure keep the standard page selected and restore trampoline.
 226+ 0235 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 227+ 0237 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 228+ 023A 21 BA 04             ld      hl, rom_return_trampoline_code
 229+ 023D 11 00 40             ld      de, ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
 230+ 0240 01 09 00             ld      bc, rom_return_trampoline_end - rom_return_trampoline_code
 231+ 0243 ED B0                ldir
 232+ 0245
 233+ 0245              .pg1_map_test_done:
 234+ 0245 3E 01                ld      a, C_DMA_ZX
 235+ 0247 D3 1B                out     (DMA_MOD), a
 236+ 0249
 237+ 0249 3E 11                ld      a, #11
 238+ 024B D3 1C                out     (DMA_HAD), a            ; physical address bits 21:16 = #11
 239+ 024D AF                   xor     a
 240+ 024E D3 1D                out     (DMA_MAD), a            ; physical address bits 15:8 = 0
 241+ 0250 AF                   xor     a
 242+ 0251 D3 1E                out     (DMA_LAD), a            ; physical address bits 7:0 = 0
 243+ 0253
 244+ 0253 3E 80                ld      a, #80
 245+ 0255 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; DMA enable = bit 7
 246+ 0257
 247+ 0257 AF                   xor     a
 248+ 0258 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; host may now start LDIR
 249+ 025A
 250+ 025A 16 08                ld      d, DMA_WAIT_OUTER
 251+ 025C              .dma_wait_outer:
 252+ 025C 01 FF FF             ld      bc, DMA_WAIT_LIMIT
 253+ 025F              .dma_wait_host_end:
 254+ 025F DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 255+ 0261 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
 256+ 0263 20 1F                jr      nz, .dma_host_end
 257+ 0265 0B                   dec     bc
 258+ 0266 78                   ld      a, b
 259+ 0267 B1                   or      c
 260+ 0268 20 F5                jr      nz, .dma_wait_host_end
 261+ 026A 15                   dec     d
 262+ 026B 20 EF                jr      nz, .dma_wait_outer
 263+ 026D
 264+ 026D                      ; Timeout: close DMA, restore PG1 and release the host command line.
 265+ 026D AF                   xor     a
 266+ 026E D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a
 267+ 0270 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 268+ 0272 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 269+ 0274 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore page 1 on DMA timeout
 270+ 0277 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 271+ 0279 32 B2 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 272+ 027C 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 273+ 027E 32 04 0E             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 274+ 0281 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 275+ 0284
 276+ 0284              .dma_host_end:
 277+ 0284 AF                   xor     a
 278+ 0285 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; disable DMA before leaving handler
 279+ 0287 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; acknowledge host's #FF marker
 280+ 0289 AF                   xor     a
 281+ 028A 32 B2 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 282+ 028D C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 283+ 0290              neogs_dma_handler_end:
 284+ 0290
 285+ 0290              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 286+ 0290              ; CMD_RUN_X86 — execute the length-framed payload beginning at $4002.
 287+ 0290              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 288+ 0290              command_run_x86:
 289+ 0290 CD 9E 02             call    execute_neogs_x86
 290+ 0293 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 291+ 0295 CD AD 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore firmware/trampoline page
 292+ 0298 AF                   xor     a
 293+ 0299 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 294+ 029B C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 295+ 029E
 296+ 029E              execute_neogs_x86:
 297+ 029E 3A B2 04             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 298+ 02A1 B7                   or      a
 299+ 02A2 28 0C                jr      z, .dma_ok
 300+ 02A4 21 FF FF             ld      hl, $FFFF
 301+ 02A7 22 B8 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 302+ 02AA 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 303+ 02AC 32 04 0E             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 304+ 02AF C9                   ret
 305+ 02B0
 306+ 02B0              .dma_ok:
 307+ 02B0                      ; Emit a live NeoGS-side dump before interpreting the DMA payload.
 308+ 02B0                      ; The host drains this stream and mirrors it to emulator port #EF.
 309+ 02B0 CD 0C 04             call    neo_debug_dump_dma_frame
 310+ 02B3
 311+ 02B3                      ; DMA frame begins with a little-endian x86 byte length at $4000.
 312+ 02B3 2A 00 40             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 313+ 02B6 7C                   ld      a, h
 314+ 02B7 B5                   or      l
 315+ 02B8 CA ED 02             jp      z, .invalid_x86_length
 316+ 02BB
 317+ 02BB                      ; Maximum code length is $3FFE: $4002 + length must not exceed $8000.
 318+ 02BB 7C                   ld      a, h
 319+ 02BC FE 3F                cp      #3F
 320+ 02BE 38 09                jr      c, .x86_length_valid
 321+ 02C0 C2 ED 02             jp      nz, .invalid_x86_length
 322+ 02C3 7D                   ld      a, l
 323+ 02C4 FE FF                cp      #FF
 324+ 02C6 D2 ED 02             jp      nc, .invalid_x86_length
 325+ 02C9
 326+ 02C9              .x86_length_valid:
 327+ 02C9 11 02 40             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 328+ 02CC 19                   add     hl, de
 329+ 02CD 22 02 0E             ld      (x86_program_end), hl
 330+ 02D0
 331+ 02D0 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 332+ 02D3 22 B6 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 333+ 02D6 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 334+ 02D9 22 00 0E             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
 335+ 02DC AF                   xor     a
 336+ 02DD 32 04 0E             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 337+ 02E0
 338+ 02E0 01 02 40             ld      bc, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 339+ 02E3 CD CE 0A             call    x86_execute_loop
 340+ 02E6

 590+ 0483
 591+ 0483              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 592+ 0483              ; neo_send_data_byte
 593+ 0483              ; IN: A = byte to send to Spectrum.
 594+ 0483              ; Wait for DBIT=0, write ZXDATWR (#03), then wait for host read to clear DBIT.
 595+ 0483              ; OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on timeout. Preserves HL and DE.
 596+ 0483              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 597+ 0483              neo_send_data_byte:
 598+ 0483 32 B1 04             ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
 599+ 0486 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_WAIT_LIMIT
 600+ 0489              .tx_wait_slot:
 601+ 0489 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 602+ 048B CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 603+ 048D 28 07                jr      z, .tx_slot_free
 604+ 048F 0B                   dec     bc
 605+ 0490 78                   ld      a, b
 606+ 0491 B1                   or      c
 607+ 0492 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_wait_slot
 608+ 0494 37                   scf
 609+ 0495 C9                   ret
 610+ 0496
 611+ 0496              .tx_slot_free:
 612+ 0496 3A B1 04             ld      a, (neo_tx_byte)
 613+ 0499 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
 614+ 049B
 615+ 049B 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_WAIT_LIMIT
 616+ 049E              .tx_wait_host_read:
 617+ 049E DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 618+ 04A0 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 619+ 04A2 28 07                jr      z, .tx_host_read
 620+ 04A4 0B                   dec     bc
 621+ 04A5 78                   ld      a, b
 622+ 04A6 B1                   or      c
 623+ 04A7 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_wait_host_read
 624+ 04A9 37                   scf
 625+ 04AA C9                   ret
 626+ 04AB
 627+ 04AB              .tx_host_read:
 628+ 04AB B7                   or      a
 629+ 04AC C9                   ret
 630+ 04AD
 631+ 04AD              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 632+ 04AD              ; Set PG1 through the immediate-port Z80 instruction.
 633+ 04AD              ; OUT (n),A forms the 16-bit I/O address as A:n. Here n=#21, so the
 634+ 04AD              ; low six address bits decode PG1. The FPGA port-enable logic checks
 635+ 04AD              ; address bits A7:A6 (from n), not the high byte containing page data.
 636+ 04AD              ; Thus #44 and #03 are both valid data values for this port.
 637+ 04AD              ; Input: A = PG1 page value. Preserves A and all other registers.
 638+ 04AD              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 639+ 04AD              neo_set_pg1:
 640+ 04AD D3 21                out     (PG1), a
 641+ 04AF C9                   ret

 653+ 04BA              ; but the following JP is still fetched from RAM because PC remains in $4000.
 654+ 04BA              rom_return_trampoline_code:
 655+ 04BA DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)

# file opened: x86_memory.asm
   1++04C3              ; =========================================================================
   2++04C3              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 physical memory backend for NeoGS GS105a
   3++04C3              ;
   4++04C3              ; x86 physical $00000-$FFFFF maps to NeoGS RAM pages 4..67
   5++04C3              ; (NeoGS physical base $010000, page size $4000 / 16 KiB).
   6++04C3              ; Window C000-FFFF is switched through MPAGEX. This keeps firmware in
   7++04C3              ; window 0 / page 0 and the current DMA frame in window 1 / page 3.
   8++04C3              ;
   9++04C3              ; x86_mem_addr is a 20-bit little-endian address:
  10++04C3              ;   +0 = bits 7:0, +1 = bits 15:8, +2 = bits 19:16 (valid 0..0F).
  11++04C3              ;
  12++04C3              ; API:
  13++04C3              ;   x86_mem_init: enable NOROM + EXPAG, preserving other GSCFG0 bits.
  14++04C3              ;   x86_mem_read8:  IN x86_mem_addr; OUT A=value, carry=invalid address.
  15++04C3              ;   x86_mem_write8: IN x86_mem_addr and x86_mem_value; carry=invalid address.
  16++04C3              ;   x86_mem_test: destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
  17++04C3              ;                 status=0, AX=0001 on pass; status=5, AX=phase:page on fail.
  18++04C3              ;
  19++04C3              ; The test leaves NOROM+EXPAG enabled. Only window 3 is switched by MPAGEX.
  20++04C3              ; =========================================================================
  21++04C3
  22++04C3              X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE EQU 4
  23++04C3              X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT   EQU 64
  24++04C3
  25++04C3              x86_mem_init:
  26++04C3 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  27++04C5 F6 09                or      M_NOROM | M_EXPAG
  28++04C7 D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  29++04C9 C9                   ret
  30++04CA
  31++04CA              ; Convert x86_mem_addr into a Z80 pointer in C000-FFFF.
  32++04CA              ; OUT: HL=window pointer, carry clear; carry set if address > FFFFF.
  33++04CA              x86_mem_map_addr:
  34++04CA 3A 12 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+2)
  35++04CD FE 10                cp      #10
  36++04CF 30 1E                jr      nc, .bad_address
  37++04D1
  38++04D1                      ; page = (physical >> 14) + 4
  39++04D1                      ; page index = (high_nibble << 2) | (middle_byte >> 6)
  40++04D1 87                   add     a, a
  41++04D2 87                   add     a, a
  42++04D3 47                   ld      b, a
  43++04D4 3A 11 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  44++04D7 07                   rlca
  45++04D8 07                   rlca
  46++04D9 E6 03                and     #03
  47++04DB B0                   or      b
  48++04DC C6 04                add     a, X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE
  49++04DE
  50++04DE                      ; MPAGEX uses ROL8(page,1); send ROR8(page,1).
  51++04DE 0F                   rrca
  52++04DF D3 10                out     (MPAGEX), a
  53++04E1
  54++04E1                      ; Window C000-FFFF maps the selected 16 KiB physical page.
  55++04E1 3A 11 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  56++04E4 E6 3F                and     #3F
  57++04E6 F6 C0                or      #C0
  58++04E8 67                   ld      h, a
  59++04E9 3A 10 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr)
  60++04EC 6F                   ld      l, a
  61++04ED B7                   or      a
  62++04EE C9                   ret
  63++04EF
  64++04EF              .bad_address:
  65++04EF 37                   scf
  66++04F0 C9                   ret
  67++04F1
  68++04F1              ; Read one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2]. OUT A=byte, carry on invalid address.
  69++04F1              x86_mem_read8:
  70++04F1 CD CA 04             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  71++04F4 D8                   ret     c
  72++04F5 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
  73++04F6 B7                   or      a
  74++04F7 C9                   ret
  75++04F8
  76++04F8              ; Write one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2], x86_mem_value.
  77++04F8              x86_mem_write8:
  78++04F8 CD CA 04             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  79++04FB D8                   ret     c
  80++04FC 3A 13 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_value)
  81++04FF 77                   ld      (hl), a
  82++0500 B7                   or      a
  83++0501 C9                   ret
  84++0502
  85++0502              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  86++0502              ; Destructive physical RAM mapping test.
  87++0502              ; Every page receives unique values at offsets 0000 and 3FFF. A second pass
  88++0502              ; verifies all 128 locations, detecting missing pages and page aliases.
  89++0502              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  90++0502              x86_mem_test:
  91++0502 CD C3 04             call    x86_mem_init
  92++0505 AF                   xor     a
  93++0506 32 14 07             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
  94++0509 32 15 07             ld      (x86_mem_phase), a
  95++050C
  96++050C              .write_page_loop:
  97++050C CD D5 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_start
  98++050F 3A 14 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
  99++0512 EE 5A                xor     #5A
 100++0514 32 13 07             ld      (x86_mem_value), a
 101++0517 CD F8 04             call    x86_mem_write8
 102++051A DA B3 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_write_start_fail
 103++051D
 104++051D CD F1 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_end
 105++0520 3A 14 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 106++0523 EE A5                xor     #A5
 107++0525 32 13 07             ld      (x86_mem_value), a
 108++0528 CD F8 04             call    x86_mem_write8
 109++052B DA B7 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_write_end_fail
 110++052E
 111++052E 3A 14 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 112++0531 3C                   inc     a
 113++0532 32 14 07             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
 114++0535 FE 40                cp      X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT
 115++0537 38 D3                jr      c, .write_page_loop
 116++0539
 117++0539 AF                   xor     a
 118++053A 32 14 07             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
 119++053D
 120++053D              .verify_page_loop:
 121++053D CD D5 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_start
 122++0540 CD F1 04             call    x86_mem_read8
 123++0543 DA BB 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_verify_start_fail
 124++0546 47                   ld      b, a
 125++0547 3A 14 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 126++054A EE 5A                xor     #5A
 127++054C B8                   cp      b
 128++054D C2 BB 06             jp      nz, x86_mem_test_verify_start_fail
 129++0550
 130++0550 CD F1 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_end
 131++0553 CD F1 04             call    x86_mem_read8
 132++0556 DA BF 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_verify_end_fail
 133++0559 47                   ld      b, a
 134++055A 3A 14 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 135++055D EE A5                xor     #A5
 136++055F B8                   cp      b
 137++0560 C2 BF 06             jp      nz, x86_mem_test_verify_end_fail
 138++0563
 139++0563 3A 14 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 140++0566 3C                   inc     a
 141++0567 32 14 07             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
 142++056A FE 40                cp      X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT
 143++056C 38 CF                jr      c, .verify_page_loop

 155++057C C9                   ret
 156++057D
 157++057D              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 158++057D              ; Segment:offset -> physical address.
 159++057D              ; Uses x86_mem_segment and x86_mem_offset_work; result in x86_mem_addr.
 160++057D              ; Physical address = ((segment << 4) + offset) & FFFFFh.
 161++057D              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 162++057D              x86_mem_calc_phys:
 163++057D                      ; Byte 0 = (segment low << 4) + offset low.
 164++057D 3A 16 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment)
 165++0580 07                   rlca
 166++0581 07                   rlca
 167++0582 07                   rlca
 168++0583 07                   rlca

 172++0589 32 10 07             ld      (x86_mem_addr), a
 173++058C 3E 00                ld      a, 0
 174++058E CE 00                adc     a, 0
 175++0590 32 1E 07             ld      (x86_mem_carry), a
 176++0593
 177++0593                      ; Byte 1 base = (segment low >> 4) | (segment high << 4).
 178++0593 3A 16 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment)
 179++0596 0F                   rrca
 180++0597 0F                   rrca
 181++0598 0F                   rrca
 182++0599 0F                   rrca

 187++05A1 07                   rlca
 188++05A2 07                   rlca
 189++05A3 07                   rlca
 190++05A4 B0                   or      b
 191++05A5
 192++05A5                      ; Add offset high to byte-1 base, then add byte-0 carry.
 193++05A5                      ; The two carry-outs are mutually exclusive for byte addition.
 194++05A5 47                   ld      b, a
 195++05A6 3A 1B 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_offset_work+1)
 196++05A9 80                   add     a, b
 197++05AA 57                   ld      d, a
 198++05AB 3E 00                ld      a, 0
 199++05AD CE 00                adc     a, 0
 200++05AF 32 1F 07             ld      (x86_mem_carry1), a   ; carry from base + offset high
 201++05B2
 202++05B2 7A                   ld      a, d
 203++05B3 47                   ld      b, a
 204++05B4 3A 1E 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_carry)

 215++05C7 3A 17 07             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment+1)
 216++05CA 0F                   rrca
 217++05CB 0F                   rrca
 218++05CC 0F                   rrca

 227++05DB
 228++05DB              ; Read a little-endian word at segment:offset.
 229++05DB              ; IN: x86_mem_segment, x86_mem_offset. OUT: HL=word, carry=invalid.
 230++05DB              ; The second byte uses (offset+1) modulo 65536 in the SAME segment.
 231++05DB              x86_mem_read16:
 232++05DB 2A 18 07             ld      hl, (x86_mem_offset)
 233++05DE 22 1A 07             ld      (x86_mem_offset_work), hl
 234++05E1 CD 7D 05             call    x86_mem_calc_phys

 236++05E7 D8                   ret     c
 237++05E8 5F                   ld      e, a

 243++05F3 CD F1 04             call    x86_mem_read8
 244++05F6 D8                   ret     c
 245++05F7 67                   ld      h, a
 246++05F8 6B                   ld      l, e
 247++05F9 B7                   or      a
 248++05FA C9                   ret

 251++05FB              ; IN: x86_mem_segment, x86_mem_offset, x86_mem_word_value.

 260++060D D8                   ret     c

 269++0621 C9                   ret

   1++0720              ; =========================================================================
   2++0720              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS foundation / POST, stage 1
   3++0720              ;
   4++0720              ; Establishes the guest-visible low-memory data layout for a future 8086
   5++0720              ; real-mode BIOS. This stage does not implement executable BIOS ROM or INT
   6++0720              ; services. IVT is initialized to zero; vectors are installed only after the
   7++0720              ; CPU INT/IRET path and guest stack semantics are implemented.
   8++0720              ;
   9++0720              ; Physical memory profile:
  10++0720              ;   00000-003FF  IVT (256 entries, 4 bytes each)
  11++0720              ;   00400-004FF  BIOS Data Area (BDA)
  12++0720              ;   00500-9FFFF  conventional RAM profile (640 KiB)
  13++0720              ;   A0000-BFFFF  reserved video memory
  14++0720              ;   C0000-EFFFF  reserved option-ROM/device window
  15++0720              ;   F0000-FFFFF  reserved P124 BIOS ROM (not read-only yet)
  16++0720              ;
  17++0720              ; Entry: bios_post
  18++0720              ; Success: x86_exit_status=0, saved_x86_ax=#B105
  19++0720              ; Failure: x86_exit_status=7, saved_x86_ax=#B1xx
  20++0720              ; No DMA/IPC transport changes.
  21++0720              ; =========================================================================
  22++0720
  23++0720              BIOS_IVT_BASE             EQU #00000
  24++0720              BIOS_IVT_SIZE             EQU #00400
  25++0720              BIOS_BDA_BASE             EQU #00400

   1++096C              ; =========================================================================
   2++096C              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 16-bit ModR/M effective-address decoder foundation
   3++096C              ;
   4++096C              ; IN:  A = ModR/M byte, BC = pointer to next instruction byte.
   5++096C              ; OUT: x86_ea_is_reg, x86_ea_reg, x86_ea_offset, x86_ea_segment.
   6++096C              ;      BC advances past disp8/disp16. Carry set on truncated displacement.
   7++096C              ;
   8++096C              ; Register encoding for r/m and reg fields:
   9++096C              ;   0 AX, 1 CX, 2 DX, 3 BX, 4 SP, 5 BP, 6 SI, 7 DI.
  10++096C              ; Memory default segment is DS; forms containing BP use SS.
  11++096C              ; mod=00,r/m=110 is direct [disp16] and uses DS.
  12++096C              ; =========================================================================
  13++096C
  14++096C              x86_decode_modrm16:

   1++0ACE              ; =========================================================================
   2++0ACE              ; PROJECT 124 — minimal x86 interpreter core for NeoGS Z80
   3++0ACE              ;
   4++0ACE              ; Supported opcodes:
   5++0ACE              ;   90       NOP
   6++0ACE              ;   40       INC AX (8086 legacy one-byte register encoding)
   7++0ACE              ;   B8 iw    MOV AX, imm16
   8++0ACE              ;   F4       HLT (successful task termination)
   9++0ACE              ;
  10++0ACE              ; Unsupported opcode: status 2. Instruction budget exhausted: status 1.
  11++0ACE              ; DMA error: status 3. Out-of-range/truncated instruction: status 4.
  12++0ACE              ; x86_program_end is the exclusive logical address after the payload.
  13++0ACE              ;
  14++0ACE              ; Entry: BC = x86 IP in NeoGS Z80 logical address space; AX stored in
  15++0ACE              ;        saved_z80_ax. Exit: saved_z80_ax and x86_exit_status are updated.
  16++0ACE              ; =========================================================================
  17++0ACE
  18++0ACE              x86_execute_loop:
  19++0ACE              x86_next_instruction:
  20++0ACE                      ; Every opcode fetch must remain strictly below the exclusive end.
  21++0ACE 2A 02 0E             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)
  22++0AD1 B7                   or      a
  23++0AD2 ED 42                sbc     hl, bc
  24++0AD4 DA 41 0B             jp      c, x86_ip_out_of_range
  25++0AD7 7C                   ld      a, h
  26++0AD8 B5                   or      l
  27++0AD9 CA 41 0B             jp      z, x86_ip_out_of_range
  28++0ADC
  29++0ADC 2A 00 0E             ld      hl, (x86_steps_remaining)
  30++0ADF 7C                   ld      a, h
  31++0AE0 B5                   or      l
  32++0AE1 20 09                jr      nz, .budget_ok
  33++0AE3 3E 01                ld      a, 1
  34++0AE5 32 04 0E             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  35++0AE8 2A B6 04             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  36++0AEB C9                   ret
  37++0AEC
  38++0AEC              .budget_ok:
  39++0AEC 2B                   dec     hl
  40++0AED 22 00 0E             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
  41++0AF0
  42++0AF0                      ; Fetch opcode and advance IP.
  43++0AF0 0A                   ld      a, (bc)
  44++0AF1 03                   inc     bc
  45++0AF2
  46++0AF2                      ; Resolve handler from 256-entry WORD table.
  47++0AF2 6F                   ld      l, a
  48++0AF3 26 00                ld      h, 0
  49++0AF5 29                   add     hl, hl
  50++0AF6 11 00 0C             ld      de, x86_jump_table
  51++0AF9 19                   add     hl, de
  52++0AFA 5E                   ld      e, (hl)
  53++0AFB 23                   inc     hl
  54++0AFC 56                   ld      d, (hl)
  55++0AFD
  56++0AFD                      ; Restore AX in HL and jump to the selected handler. PUSH/RET is
  57++0AFD                      ; the Z80's safe indirect-jump idiom; BC remains the virtual x86 IP.
  58++0AFD 2A B6 04             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  59++0B00 D5                   push    de
  60++0B01 C9                   ret
  61++0B02
  62++0B02              ; 90 — NOP
  63++0B02              x86_nop:
  64++0B02 22 B6 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
  65++0B05 C3 CE 0A             jp      x86_next_instruction
  66++0B08
  67++0B08              ; 40 — INC AX
  68++0B08              x86_inc_ax:
  69++0B08 23                   inc     hl
  70++0B09 22 B6 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
  71++0B0C C3 CE 0A             jp      x86_next_instruction
  72++0B0F
  73++0B0F              ; B8 iw — MOV AX, imm16 (little-endian immediate)
  74++0B0F              x86_mov_ax_imm16:
  75++0B0F                      ; B8 requires two immediate bytes. Reject a truncated instruction.
  76++0B0F 2A 02 0E             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)
  77++0B12 B7                   or      a
  78++0B13 ED 42                sbc     hl, bc
  79++0B15 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
  80++0B18 B7                   or      a
  81++0B19 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
  82++0B1B DA 41 0B             jp      c, x86_ip_out_of_range
  83++0B1E

   1++0E05              ; =========================================================================
   2++0E05              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode interrupt frame engine (Stage 2A)
   3++0E05              ;
   4++0E05              ; Implements the architectural memory/stack part of software INT and IRET.
   5++0E05              ; This module is deliberately separate from the legacy four-opcode fetch
   6++0E05              ; loop: opcode dispatch and execution of guest handler code are Stage 2B.
   7++0E05              ;
   8++0E05              ; x86_int_enter:
   9++0E05              ;   IN: A = interrupt vector number
  10++0E05              ;   Uses x86_reg_{ss,sp,cs,ip,flags}.
  11++0E05              ;   Push order: FLAGS, CS, IP; final SP points to saved IP.
  12++0E05              ;   Loads new IP:CS from IVT[vector], clears IF and TF.
  13++0E05              ;   OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on memory error.
  14++0E05              ;
  15++0E05              ; x86_iret_return:
  16++0E05              ;   Pops IP, CS, FLAGS; restores all three and advances SP by six.
  17++0E05              ;
  18++0E05              ; x86_int_self_test:
  19++0E05              ;   Installs a temporary vector 20h, validates the exact stack frame,
  20++0E05              ;   validates IF/TF clearing, executes IRET, validates restored state,
  21++0E05              ;   and removes the temporary vector.
  22++0E05              ;   Success: status=0, AX=1A01h. Failure: status=8, AX=1Axxh.
  23++0E05              ; =========================================================================
  24++0E05
  25++0E05              X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK EQU #FC
  26++0E05
  27++0E05              x86_int_enter:
  28++0E05 32 22 10             ld      (x86_int_vector), a
  29++0E08
  30++0E08                      ; IVT entry address = vector * 4, physical segment zero.
  31++0E08 6F                   ld      l, a
  32++0E09 26 00                ld      h, 0
  33++0E0B 29                   add     hl, hl
  34++0E0C 29                   add     hl, hl
  35++0E0D 22 18 07             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  36++0E10 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  37++0E13 22 16 07             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  38++0E16 CD DB 05             call    x86_mem_read16
  39++0E19 D8                   ret     c
  40++0E1A 22 25 10             ld      (x86_int_target_ip), hl
  41++0E1D
  42++0E1D 2A 18 07             ld      hl, (x86_mem_offset)
  43++0E20 23                   inc     hl
  44++0E21 23                   inc     hl
  45++0E22 22 18 07             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  46++0E25 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  47++0E28 22 16 07             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  48++0E2B CD DB 05             call    x86_mem_read16
  49++0E2E D8                   ret     c
  50++0E2F 22 27 10             ld      (x86_int_target_cs), hl
  51++0E32
  52++0E32                      ; 8086 interrupt entry pushes FLAGS, then CS, then return IP.
  53++0E32 ED 5B C2 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_flags)
  54++0E36 CD 77 0E             call    x86_int_push_word
  55++0E39 D8                   ret     c
  56++0E3A ED 5B BA 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_cs)
  57++0E3E CD 77 0E             call    x86_int_push_word
  58++0E41 D8                   ret     c
  59++0E42 ED 5B C0 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ip)
  60++0E46 CD 77 0E             call    x86_int_push_word
  61++0E49 D8                   ret     c
  62++0E4A
  63++0E4A                      ; IF (bit 9) and TF (bit 8) are cleared in the live FLAGS register.
  64++0E4A 3A C3 0A             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags+1)
  65++0E4D E6 FC                and     X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK
  66++0E4F 32 C3 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags+1), a
  67++0E52 2A 25 10             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_ip)
  68++0E55 22 C0 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  69++0E58 2A 27 10             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_cs)
  70++0E5B 22 BA 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  71++0E5E B7                   or      a
  72++0E5F C9                   ret
  73++0E60
  74++0E60              x86_iret_return:
  75++0E60 CD 97 0E             call    x86_int_pop_word
  76++0E63 D8                   ret     c
  77++0E64 22 C0 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  78++0E67
  79++0E67 CD 97 0E             call    x86_int_pop_word
  80++0E6A D8                   ret     c
  81++0E6B 22 BA 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  82++0E6E
  83++0E6E CD 97 0E             call    x86_int_pop_word
  84++0E71 D8                   ret     c
  85++0E72 22 C2 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
  86++0E75 B7                   or      a
  87++0E76 C9                   ret
  88++0E77
  89++0E77              ; IN: DE=word. Decrement guest SP by two, then write SS:SP.
  90++0E77              x86_int_push_word:
  91++0E77 ED 53 23 10          ld      (x86_int_word), de
  92++0E7B 2A B0 0A             ld      hl, (x86_reg_sp)
  93++0E7E 2B                   dec     hl
  94++0E7F 2B                   dec     hl
  95++0E80 22 B0 0A             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
  96++0E83 22 18 07             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  97++0E86 ED 5B BC 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ss)
  98++0E8A ED 53 16 07          ld      (x86_mem_segment), de
  99++0E8E 2A 23 10             ld      hl, (x86_int_word)
 100++0E91 22 1C 07             ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
 101++0E94 C3 FB 05             jp      x86_mem_write16
 102++0E97
 103++0E97              ; OUT: HL=word at SS:SP. SP advances only after a successful read.

   1++1029              ; =========================================================================
   2++1029              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode fetch/execute core, Stage 2B
   3++1029              ;
   4++1029              ; This bounded executable slice runs guest instructions through CS:IP and
   5++1029              ; the physical-memory backend. Supported: NOP, register-direct 8/16-bit
   6++1029              ; arithmetic and logical subsets, PUSHF, INC/DEC r16, PUSH/POP r16,
   7++1029              ; MOV r8/r16,imm, MOV r/m8/r/m16 register-memory forms, short Jcc,
   8++1029              ; LOOP/JCXZ, JMP rel8, HLT, INT imm8, INT3, IRET.
   9++1029              ; Arithmetic and 8/16-bit logical memory ModR/M operands support segment overrides.
  10++1029              ; It is intentionally not a general 8086 emulator.
  11++1029              ;
  12++1029              ; x86_real_mode_test installs a guest program at 0000:5002 and an interrupt
  13++1029              ; handler at 0000:5100. The guest loads all eight general registers with
  14++1029              ; MOV r16,imm16, invokes INT 20h, then resumes at HLT after handler IRET.
  15++1029              ; The test checks all MOV destinations, interrupt return, FLAGS and SP.
  16++1029              ; Success: status=0, AX=1A02h. Failures: status=9/11 with stage-specific AX codes.
  17++1029              ; =========================================================================
  18++1029

 763++15E3
 764++15E3              ; 40-47 INC r16 and 48-4F DEC r16.
 765++15E3              ; The register file is contiguous in 8086 encoding order (AX,CX,DX,BX,
 766++15E3              ; SP,BP,SI,DI). INC/DEC preserve CF and all non-status/control FLAGS bits.
 767++15E3              x86_real_inc_r16:

   1++184B              ; =========================================================================
   2++184B              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode 16-bit ALU (register and memory ModR/M)
   3++184B              ;
   4++184B              ; Supported 16-bit ModR/M forms:
   5++184B              ; ADD 01/03, ADC 11/13, SBB 19/1B, SUB 29/2B, CMP 39/3B.
   6++184B              ; Register and memory operands are supported in both directions. Memory
   7++184B              ; effective addresses and segment overrides use the shared MOV decoder.
   8++184B              ; 8-bit arithmetic and logical memory forms remain unsupported.
   9++184B              ; PUSHF 9C is included to make guest FLAGS assertions observable.
  10++184B              ; =========================================================================

  12++2F6F              ; INT 10h enters through the normal IVT/stack frame. The BIOS service itself
  13++2F6F              ; is a firmware trap, then returns through the normal IRET primitive.
  14++2F6F              ; Host stream protocol over DBIT/GSDAT:
  15++2F6F              ;   nonzero byte = teletype character
  16++2F6F              ;   00 00        = clear the lower debug console
  17++2F6F              ;   00 01 row col= set lower debug console cursor
  18++2F6F              ; =========================================================================
  19++2F6F
  20++2F6F              BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS EQU 42
  21++2F6F              BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS    EQU 12
  22++2F6F              BIOS_VIDEO_MODE    EQU #03
  23++2F6F
  24++2F6F              bios_int10_dispatch:
  25++2F6F 2A A8 0A             ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
  26++2F72 7C                   ld      a, h
  27++2F73 FE 00                cp      #00
  28++2F75 CA B9 2F             jp      z, bios_int10_set_mode
  29++2F78 FE 0F                cp      #0F
  30++2F7A CA 18 30             jp      z, bios_int10_get_mode
  31++2F7D FE 02                cp      #02
  32++2F7F CA 24 30             jp      z, bios_int10_set_cursor
  33++2F82 FE 03                cp      #03
  34++2F84 CA 5E 30             jp      z, bios_int10_get_cursor
  35++2F87 FE 0E                cp      #0E
  36++2F89 CA 78 30             jp      z, bios_int10_teletype
  37++2F8C B7                   or      a
  38++2F8D C9                   ret
  39++2F8E

  49++2FA3 D8                   ret     c
  50++2FA4 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0

  55++2FB3 22 1C 07             ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
  56++2FB6 C3 FB 05             jp      x86_mem_write16

  81++2FE3 D8                   ret     c

  84++2FE8 CD FC 30             call    bios_video_write_byte
  85++2FEB D8                   ret     c
  86++2FEC 21 84 04             ld      hl, #0484

  11  33AD              ; Firmware occupies $0000-$33FF at most. Keep at least $0C00 bytes below SP=$3FFF
  12  33AD              ; for the Z80 stack; x86 DMA payload remains at $5000.

  18  33AD              ; GS105a #14 writes the executable bootstrap into fixed RAM $4000-$7FFF.
  19  33AD                      ORG     $4000
  20  4000                      INCLUDE "bootstrap.asm"
# file opened: bootstrap.asm
   1+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 4000              ; PROJECT 124 — first-stage NeoGS executable bootstrap
   3+ 4000              ;
   4+ 4000              ; GS105a command #14 (RECEIVE BLOCK) loads this raw Z80 image into fixed RAM
   5+ 4000              ; at $4000. GS105a command #13 (JUMP TO ADDRESS) enters it at $4000.
   6+ 4000              ;
   7+ 4000              ; The bootstrap maps RAM over the low ROM window, receives a 16-bit firmware
   8+ 4000              ; length and exactly that many bytes through the documented ZX data channel,
   9+ 4000              ; stores the custom firmware at RAM address $0000, then jumps to $0000.
  10+ 4000              ;
  11+ 4000              ; It executes from the fixed RAM window $4000-$7FFF and keeps its stack below
  12+ 4000              ; $4000. The fixed $4000-$7FFF RAM window remains visible after B_NOROM is set.
  13+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
  14+ 4000
  15+ 4000              neogs_bootstrap_start:
  16+ 4000 F3                   di
  17+ 4001 31 FF 3F             ld      sp, #3FFF
  18+ 4004
  19+ 4004                      ; Hide the stock ROM and expose RAM at logical $0000-$3FFF.
  20+ 4004 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  21+ 4006 E6 FD                and     #FD                    ; clear RAMRO: low RAM must be writable
  22+ 4008 F6 01                or      M_NOROM                ; map RAM over the stock ROM at $0000
  23+ 400A D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  24+ 400C
  25+ 400C                      ; Tell Spectrum that the tiny loader is running and waiting for length.
  26+ 400C 3E A1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
  27+ 400E CD 8B 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  28+ 4011
  29+ 4011                      ; Host sends length low byte, then high byte.
  30+ 4011 CD 77 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  31+ 4014 5F                   ld      e, a
  32+ 4015 CD 77 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  33+ 4018 57                   ld      d, a
  34+ 4019
  35+ 4019                      ; Reject zero length and any image larger than $3400, preserving a 3 KiB stack reserve.
  36+ 4019 7A                   ld      a, d
  37+ 401A B3                   or      e
  38+ 401B 28 22                jr      z, bootstrap_error
  39+ 401D 7A                   ld      a, d
  40+ 401E FE 34                cp      #34
  41+ 4020 38 06                jr      c, bootstrap_length_ok
  42+ 4022 20 1B                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  43+ 4024 7B                   ld      a, e
  44+ 4025 B7                   or      a
  45+ 4026 20 17                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  46+ 4028
  47+ 4028              bootstrap_length_ok:
  48+ 4028                      ; Explicitly acknowledge that the length is accepted before the host
  49+ 4028                      ; starts streaming the firmware payload.
  50+ 4028 3E A3                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
  51+ 402A CD 8B 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  52+ 402D 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  53+ 4030
  54+ 4030              bootstrap_copy_loop:
  55+ 4030 7A                   ld      a, d
  56+ 4031 B3                   or      e
  57+ 4032 28 08                jr      z, bootstrap_start_firmware
  58+ 4034 CD 77 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  59+ 4037 77                   ld      (hl), a
  60+ 4038 23                   inc     hl
  61+ 4039 1B                   dec     de
  62+ 403A 18 F4                jr      bootstrap_copy_loop
  63+ 403C
  64+ 403C              bootstrap_start_firmware:
  65+ 403C C3 00 00             jp      0
  66+ 403F
  67+ 403F              bootstrap_error:
  68+ 403F 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  69+ 4041 CD 8B 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  70+ 4044 C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
  71+ 4047
  72+ 4047              ; Terminal state used when the host stops responding or cannot consume data.
  73+ 4047              ; Never leave a bootstrap polling loop running forever with no timeout.
  74+ 4047              bootstrap_halt:
  75+ 4047 F3                   di
  76+ 4048              .bootstrap_halt_loop:
  77+ 4048 76                   halt
  78+ 4049 18 FD                jr      .bootstrap_halt_loop
  79+ 404B
  80+ 404B              ; Best-effort error ACK after a receive timeout. This path has its own
  81+ 404B              ; bounded waits and never calls bootstrap_send_data_byte recursively.
  82+ 404B              bootstrap_timeout:
  83+ 404B 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  84+ 404D 32 B6 40             ld      (bootstrap_tx_byte), a
  85+ 4050 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
  86+ 4053              .timeout_wait_slot:
  87+ 4053 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
  88+ 4055 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
  89+ 4057 28 08                jr      z, .timeout_slot_ready
  90+ 4059 0B                   dec     bc
  91+ 405A 78                   ld      a, b
  92+ 405B B1                   or      c
  93+ 405C 20 F5                jr      nz, .timeout_wait_slot
  94+ 405E C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
  95+ 4061              .timeout_slot_ready:
  96+ 4061 3A B6 40             ld      a, (bootstrap_tx_byte)
  97+ 4064 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
  98+ 4066 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
  99+ 4069              .timeout_wait_host:
 100+ 4069 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 101+ 406B CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 102+ 406D 28 D8                jr      z, bootstrap_halt
 103+ 406F 0B                   dec     bc
 104+ 4070 78                   ld      a, b
 105+ 4071 B1                   or      c
 106+ 4072 20 F5                jr      nz, .timeout_wait_host
 107+ 4074 C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 108+ 4077
 109+ 4077              ; Wait for one byte from Spectrum. Reading ZXDATRD clears DBIT.
 110+ 4077              bootstrap_receive_data_byte:
 111+ 4077 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 112+ 407A              .rx_wait:
 113+ 407A DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 114+ 407C CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 115+ 407E 20 08                jr      nz, .rx_ready
 116+ 4080 0B                   dec     bc
 117+ 4081 78                   ld      a, b
 118+ 4082 B1                   or      c
 119+ 4083 20 F5                jr      nz, .rx_wait
 120+ 4085 C3 4B 40             jp      bootstrap_timeout
 121+ 4088              .rx_ready:
 122+ 4088 DB 02                in      a, (ZXDATRD)
 123+ 408A C9                   ret
 124+ 408B
 125+ 408B              ; Send one byte to Spectrum. Host reads GSDAT and clears DBIT.
 126+ 408B              bootstrap_send_data_byte:
 127+ 408B 32 B6 40             ld      (bootstrap_tx_byte), a
 128+ 408E 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 129+ 4091              .tx_free:
 130+ 4091 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 131+ 4093 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 132+ 4095 28 08                jr      z, .tx_slot_free
 133+ 4097 0B                   dec     bc
 134+ 4098 78                   ld      a, b
 135+ 4099 B1                   or      c
 136+ 409A 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_free
 137+ 409C C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 138+ 409F              .tx_slot_free:
 139+ 409F 3A B6 40             ld      a, (bootstrap_tx_byte)
 140+ 40A2 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
 141+ 40A4 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 142+ 40A7              .tx_wait_read:
 143+ 40A7 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 144+ 40A9 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 145+ 40AB 28 08                jr      z, .tx_done
 146+ 40AD 0B                   dec     bc
 147+ 40AE 78                   ld      a, b
 148+ 40AF B1                   or      c
 149+ 40B0 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_wait_read
 150+ 40B2 C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 151+ 40B5              .tx_done:
 152+ 40B5 C9                   ret
 153+ 40B6
 154+ 40B6              bootstrap_tx_byte:
 155+ 40B6 00                   db      0
 156+ 40B7
 157+ 40B7              neogs_bootstrap_end:
 158+ 40B7
# file closed: bootstrap.asm

  23  40B7                      SAVEBIN "neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
  24  40B7                      SAVEBIN "zx_main/neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
